  │ 143 +     def compute_merkle_root(self) -> str:                        │
  │ 144 +         """Compute merkle root hash of all files."""             │
  │ 145 +         if not self.files:                                       │
  │ 146 +             return hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest()               │
  │ 147 +                                                                  │
  │ 148 +         # Sort files by path for deterministic hashing           │
  │ 149 +         sorted_files = sorted(self.files, key=lambda f: f.relat… │
  │ 150 +         hashes = [f.sha256.encode() for f in sorted_files]       │
  │ 151 +                                                                  │
  │ 152 +         # Compute merkle tree                                    │
  │ 153 +         while len(hashes) > 1:                                   │
  │ 154 +             if len(hashes) % 2 == 1:                             │
  │ 155 +                 hashes.append(hashes[-1])  # Duplicate last for… │
  │ 156 +             hashes = [                                           │
  │ 157 +                 hashlib.sha256(hashes[i] + hashes[i + 1]).diges… │
  │ 158 +                 for i in range(0, len(hashes), 2)                │
  │ 159 +             ]                                                    │
  │ 160 +                                                                  │
  │ 161 +         return hashes[0].hex()                                   │
  │ 162 +                                                                  │
  │ 163 +     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:                         │
  │ 164 +         return {                                                 │
  │ 165 +             "manifest": self.manifest.to_dict(),                 │
  │ 166 +             "files": [                                           │
  │ 167 +                 {                                                │
  │ 168 +                     "relative_path": f.relative_path,            │
  │ 169 +                     "sha256": f.sha256,                          │
  │ 170 +                     "size_bytes": f.size_bytes,                  │

  │ 193 +                                                                  │
  │ 194 +     def compute_file_hash(self, file_path: Path) -> str:         │
  │ 195 +         """Compute SHA-256 hash of file contents."""             │
  │ 196 +         sha256 = hashlib.sha256()                                │
  │ 197 +         with open(file_path, "rb") as f:                         │
  │ 198 +             for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b""):        │
  │ 199 +                 sha256.update(chunk)                             │
  │ 200 +         return sha256.hexdigest()                                │

  │ 325 +     def classify_repository(                                     │
  │ 326 +         self,                                                    │

  │ 328 +         category: RepoCategory,                                  │

  │ 374 +             return "MAXIMAL"                                     │
  │ 375 +         elif category in [RepoCategory.ENTERPRISE, RepoCategory… │
  │ 376 +             return "HIGH"                                        │
  │ 377 +         else:                                                    │
  │ 378 +             return "STANDARD"                                    │

  │ 184 +         """Compute merkle root for this domain."""               │

  │ 186 +             "domain_id": self.domain_id,                         │

  │ 192 +         }, sort_keys=True)                                       │

  │ 194 +         return self.merkle_root                                  │

  │ 492 +     def create_domain(                                           │
  │ 493 +         self,                                                    │

  │ 509 +         )                                                        │
  │ 510 +                                                                  │
  │ 511 +         # Link to parent domains                                 │
  │ 512 +         for parent_id in domain.parent_domains:                  │
  │ 513 +             if parent_id in self.domains:                        │
  │ 514 +                 self.domains[parent_id].child_domains.append(do… │
  │ 515 +                                                                  │
  │ 516 +         self.domains[domain_id] = domain                         │
  │ 517 +         logger.info(f"Created domain: {domain_id}")              │
  │ 518 +         return domain                                            │
  │ 519 +                                                                  │
  │ 520 +     def add_repository_to_domain(self, domain_id: str, repo_id:… │
  │ 521 +         """Associate a repository with a domain."""              │
  │ 522 +         if domain_id not in self.domains:                        │
  │ 523 +             raise ValueError(f"Domain {domain_id} not found")    │
  │ 524 +         self.domains[domain_id].repositories.append(repo_id)     │
  │ 525 +         self.domains[domain_id].modified_at = datetime.now(time… │
  │ 526 +                                                                  │
  │ 527 +     def classify_issue(                                          │
  │ 528 +         self,                                                    │
  │ 529 +         issue_id: str,                                           │
  │ 530 +         category: IssueCategory,                                 │
  │ 531 +         severity: str,                                           │
  │ 532 +         domain_id: str                                           │
  │ 533 +     ) -> Dict[str, Any]:                                         │
  │ 534 +         """Classify an issue within the taxonomy."""             │
  │ 535 +         classification = {                                       │
  │ 536 +             "issue_id": issue_id,                                │
  │ 537 +             "category": category.value,                          │
  │ 538 +             "severity": severity,                                │
  │ 539 +             "domain_id": domain_id,                              │
  │ 540 +             "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), │
  │ 541 +         }                                                        │
  │ 542 +                                                                  │
  │ 543 +         if category.value not in self.issue_taxonomy:            │
  │ 544 +             self.issue_taxonomy[category.value] = []             │
  │ 545 +         self.issue_taxonomy[category.value].append(issue_id)     │
  │ 546 +                                                                  │
  │ 547 +         return classification                                    │
  │ 548 +                                                                  │
  │ 549 +     def save_expansion(self) -> None:                            │
  │ 550 +         """Save the domain expansion to disk."""                 │
  │ 551 +         # Save domains                                           │
  │ 552 +         domains_path = self.base_path / "expanded_domains.json"  │
  │ 553 +         domains_data = {                                         │
  │ 554 +             "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoforma… │
  │ 555 +             "total_domains": len(self.domains),                  │
  │ 556 +             "domains": {                                         │
  │ 557 +                 domain_id: {                                     │
  │ 558 +                     "domain_id": d.domain_id,                    │
  │ 559 +                     "name": d.name,                              │
  │ 560 +                     "layer": d.layer.name,                       │
  │ 561 +                     "domain_type": d.domain_type.value,          │
  │ 562 +                     "description": d.description,                │
  │ 563 +                     "parent_domains": d.parent_domains,          │
  │ 564 +                     "child_domains": d.child_domains,            │
  │ 565 +                     "adjacent_domains": d.adjacent_domains,      │
  │ 566 +                     "repositories": d.repositories,              │
  │ 567 +                     "invariants": d.invariants,                  │
  │ 568 +                     "merkle_root": d.compute_merkle_root(),      │
  │ 569 +                 }                                                │
  │ 570 +                 for domain_id, d in self.domains.items()         │
  │ 571 +             }                                                    │
  │ 572 +         }                                                        │
  │ 573 +         with open(domains_path, "w") as f:                       │
  │ 574 +             json.dump(domains_data, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True) │
  │ 575 +         logger.info(f"Saved {len(self.domains)} domains to {dom… │
  │ 576 +                                                                  │
  │ 577 +         # Save type constructors                                 │
  │ 578 +         types_path = self.base_path / "type_constructors.json"   │
  │ 579 +         types_data = {                                           │
  │ 580 +             "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoforma… │
  │ 581 +             "constructors": {                                    │
  │ 582 +                 name: {                                          │
  │ 583 +                     "name": tc.name,                             │
  │ 584 +                     "arity": tc.arity,                           │
  │ 585 +                     "parameters": tc.parameters,                 │
  │ 586 +                     "constraints": tc.constraints,               │
  │ 587 +                     "documentation": tc.documentation,           │
  │ 588 +                 }                                                │
  │ 589 +                 for name, tc in self.type_constructors.items()   │
  │ 590 +             }                                                    │
  │ 591 +         }                                                        │
  │ 592 +         with open(types_path, "w") as f:                         │
  │ 593 +             json.dump(types_data, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)   │
  │ 594 +         logger.info(f"Saved {len(self.type_constructors)} type … │
  │ 595 +                                                                  │
  │ 596 +         # Save issue taxonomy                                    │
  │ 597 +         issues_path = self.base_path / "issue_taxonomy.json"     │
  │ 598 +         issues_data = {                                          │
  │ 599 +             "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoforma… │
  │ 600 +             "categories": {cat.value: {} for cat in IssueCatego… │
  │ 601 +             "taxonomy": self.issue_taxonomy,                     │
  │ 602 +         }                                                        │

  │  22 + class InfrastructureLayer(Enum):                                 │
  │  23 +     """Layer classification for city infrastructure."""          │
  │  24 +     REGIONAL = "regional"          # Multi-city / County         │
  │  25 +     CITYWIDE = "citywide"          # Entire municipality         │
  │  26 +     DISTRICT = "district"          # Neighborhood/Borough        │
  │  27 +     BLOCK = "block"                # Street level                │
  │  28 +     BUILDING = "building"          # Individual structures       │
  │  29 +     SUBSTRUCTURE = "substructure"  # Below ground / internal     │
  │  30 +                                                                  │
  │  31 +                                                                  │

  │ 376 +     def add_domain(self, domain: InfrastructureDomain) -> None:  │

  │ 379 +                                                                  │
  │ 380 +     def get_critical_infrastructure(self) -> List[Infrastructur… │
  │ 381 +         """Get all critical infrastructure domains."""           │
  │ 382 +         return [d for d in self.domains.values() if d.criticali… │
  │ 383 +                                                                  │
  │ 384 +     def get_domains_by_category(self, category: InfrastructureC… │
  │ 385 +         """Get domains by category."""                           │
  │ 386 +         return [d for d in self.domains.values() if d.category … │
  │ 387 +                                                                  │
  │ 388 +     def analyze_dependencies(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:            │
  │ 389 +         """Analyze the dependency graph."""                      │
  │ 390 +         analysis = {                                             │
  │ 391 +             "total_domains": len(self.domains),                  │
  │ 392 +             "critical_domains": len(self.get_critical_infrastru… │
  │ 393 +             "dependency_chains": [],                             │
  │ 394 +             "single_points_of_failure": [],                      │
  │ 395 +         }                                                        │
  │ 396 +                                                                  │
  │ 397 +         # Find domains with no upstream (sources)                │
  │ 398 +         sources = [d for d in self.domains.values() if not d.up… │
  │ 399 +         analysis["source_domains"] = [d.domain_id for d in sour… │
  │ 400 +                                                                  │
  │ 401 +         # Find domains with many downstream (critical hubs)      │
  │ 402 +         hub_domains = sorted(                                    │
  │ 403 +             self.domains.values(),                               │
  │ 404 +             key=lambda d: len(d.downstream_dependencies),        │
  │ 405 +             reverse=True                                         │
  │ 406 +         )[:5]                                                    │
  │ 407 +         analysis["critical_hubs"] = [                            │
  │ 408 +             {"domain": d.domain_id, "downstream_count": len(d.d… │
  │ 409 +             for d in hub_domains                                 │
  │ 410 +         ]                                                        │
  │ 411 +                                                                  │
  │ 412 +         return analysis                                          │
  │ 413 +                                                                  │
  │ 414 +     def export_to_json(self, path: Path) -> None:                │
  │ 415 +         """Export the infrastructure system to JSON."""          │
  │ 416 +         data = {                                                 │
  │ 417 +             "city_name": self.city_name,                         │
  │ 418 +             "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoforma… │
  │ 419 +             "total_domains": len(self.domains),                  │
  │ 420 +             "domains": {                                         │
  │ 421 +                 domain_id: {                                     │
  │ 422 +                     "domain_id": d.domain_id,                    │
  │ 423 +                     "name": d.name,                              │
  │ 424 +                     "category": d.category.value,                │
  │ 425 +                     "layer": d.layer.value,                      │

  │  64 + ## Case Study Categories (10 categories, 50 repos each = 500 to… │
  │  65 +                                                                  │
  │  66 + | Category | ID Prefix | Target Count | Description |            │
  │  67 + |----------|-----------|-------------|-------------|             │
  │  68 + | Game Mods | CS_GMOD | 50 | Minecraft, Factorio, Skyrim, GTA, … │
  │  69 + | ML Research | CS_ML | 50 | PyTorch, TensorFlow, HuggingFace, … │
  │  70 + | Enterprise | CS_ENT | 50 | Kubernetes, databases, message que… │
  │  71 + | Web Apps | CS_WEB | 50 | React, Next.js, Django, Rails |       │
  │  72 + | Systems | CS_SYS | 50 | Linux kernel, OpenSSL, glibc |         │
  │  73 + | AI Agents | CS_AI | 50 | LangChain, AutoGPT, CrewAI |          │
  │  74 + | Compilers | CS_COMP | 50 | GCC, Rust, LLVM, TypeScript |       │
  │  75 + | Databases | CS_DB | 50 | PostgreSQL, SQLite, Redis, MongoDB |  │
  │  76 + | Networking | CS_NET | 50 | nginx, gRPC, TCP/IP, DNS |          │
  │  77 + | Mobile | CS_MOB | 50 | Android, iOS, React Native, Flutter |   │
  │  78 +                                                                  │
  │  79 + ## Per-Case-Study Deliverables                                   │
  │  80 +                                                                  │
  │  81 + Each case study directory contains exactly 4 files:              │
  │  82 +                                                                  │
  │  83 + 1. `gap_analysis.json` — Structured forensic analysis            │
  │  84 +    - issue URL, repository, language, framework                  │
  │  85 +    - root cause (verbatim code quotes with file:line)            │
  │  86 +    - invariant violations (which domain invariants are broken)   │
  │  87 +    - affected components (list of files/classes/functions)       │
  │  88 +    - fix proposal (code diff or pseudocode)                      │
  │  89 +    - falsification test (condition under which fix is wrong)     │
  │  90 +    - SHA-256 hash of the analysis                                │
  │  91 +                                                                  │
  │  92 + 2. `pr_description.md` — Copy-paste ready GitHub comment         │
  │  93 +    - Root Cause section (with code quotes)                       │
  │  94 +    - Fix section (with diff)                                     │
  │  95 +    - Why This Works section                                      │
  │  96 +    - Testing section                                             │
  │  97 +    - Secular projection only                                     │
  │  98 +                                                                  │
  │  99 + 3. `test_specification.md` — Falsification test cases            │
  │ 100 +    - Positive tests (fix works)                                  │
  │ 101 +    - Negative tests (original bug reproduced)                    │
  │ 102 +    - Regression tests                                            │
  │ 103 +    - Performance benchmarks (if applicable)                      │
  │ 104 +                                                                  │
  │ 105 + 4. `ATTRIBUTION.md` — License, original authors, repo URL        │
  │ 106 +    - Original repo license                                       │
  │ 107 +    - Issue author attribution                                    │
  │ 108 +    - Non-affiliation statement                                   │
  │ 109 +    - Date of analysis                                            │
  │ 110 +                                                                  │
  │ 111 + ## Mapping to Ontology                                           │
  │ 112 +                                                                  │
  │ 113 + Every case study maps to at least one domain in ontology/ontolo… │
  │ 114 + - Game mods → D_GRAPHICS, D_GAMEMODS, D_MINECRAFT_SPATIAL        │
  │ 115 + - ML research → D_AI_ONTOLOGICAL_STATUS, D_PATTERN_RECOGNITION   │
  │ 116 + - Enterprise → D_CORPORATE_COMPLIANCE, D_ISO_STANDARDS           │
  │ 117 + - Web apps → D_PLATFORM, D_WEBSEC                                │
  │ 118 + - Systems → D_CRYPTO, D_AEROSPACE                                │
  │ 119 + - AI agents → D_AI_ONTOLOGICAL_STATUS, D_ETHICS                  │
  │ 120 + - Compilers → D_COMPUTABILITY, D_FORMAL_LANGUAGES                │
  │ 121 + - Databases → D_FINANCIAL, D_PRIVACY_LAW                         │
  │ 122 + - Networking → D_TELECOMMUNICATIONS_LAW, D_CRYPTO                │
  │ 123 + - Mobile → D_CONSUMER_PROTECTION, D_DISABILITY_RIGHTS            │
  │ 124 +                                                                  │
  │ 125 + ## Quality Gates                                                 │
  │ 126 +                                                                  │
  │ 127 + 1. EVIDENCE GATE: Every claim backed by verbatim code quote      │
  │ 128 + 2. FIX GATE: Every fix includes falsification condition          │
  │ 129 + 3. HASH GATE: gap_analysis.json SHA-256 recorded in INDEX        │
  │ 130 + 4. HONESTY GATE: Uncertainties marked as UNCERTAIN, not closed   │
  │ 131 +                                                                  │
  │ 132 + ## Grace Principle                                               │
  │ 133 +                                                                  │
  │ 134 + Every case study treats the original developer with grace.       │
  │ 135 + No blame. No mockery. Only: "Here is the bug. Here is why.       │
  │ 136 + Here is the fix. Here is how to test it."                        │

  │ 102 +                                                                  │
  │ 103 +     def l2_distance_squared(self, other: 'RepresentationVector'… │
  │ 104 +         """Squared L2 distance (avoids sqrt/float)."""           │
  │ 105 +         if len(self.components) != len(other.components):        │
  │ 106 +             raise ValueError(                                    │
  │ 107 +                 f"Dimension mismatch: {len(self.components)} vs… │
  │ 108 +                 f"{len(other.components)}"                       │
  │ 109 +             )                                                    │
  │ 110 +         return sum(                                              │
  │ 111 +             (a - b) * (a - b)                                    │
  │ 112 +             for a, b in zip(self.components, other.components)   │
  │ 113 +         )                                                        │

  │ 117 + class SpectralSignature:                                         │

  │ 119 +     values: Dict[SpectralBand, Fraction]                         │
  │ 120 +                                                                  │
  │ 121 +     def ndvi(self) -> Optional[Fraction]:                        │
  │ 122 +         """Normalized Difference Vegetation Index = (NIR - RED)… │
  │ 123 +         nir = self.values.get(SpectralBand.NIR)                  │
  │ 124 +         red = self.values.get(SpectralBand.RED)                  │
  │ 125 +         if nir is None or red is None:                           │
  │ 126 +             return None                                          │
  │ 127 +         denom = nir + red                                        │
  │ 128 +         if denom == 0:                                           │
  │ 129 +             return Fraction(0)                                   │
  │ 130 +         return (nir - red) / denom                               │
  │ 131 +                                                                  │
  │ 132 +     def ndwi(self) -> Optional[Fraction]:                        │
  │ 133 +         """Normalized Difference Water Index = (GREEN - NIR) / … │
  │ 134 +         green = self.values.get(SpectralBand.GREEN)              │
  │ 135 +         nir = self.values.get(SpectralBand.NIR)                  │
  │ 136 +         if green is None or nir is None:                         │
  │ 137 +             return None                                          │
  │ 138 +         denom = green + nir                                      │
  │ 139 +         if denom == 0:                                           │
  │ 140 +             return Fraction(0)                                   │
  │ 141 +         return (green - nir) / denom                             │

  │   1 + """Cryptographic verification — hash chains, Merkle proofs, com… │
  │   2 +                                                                  │
  │   3 + Extends the existing SHA-256 infrastructure with formal proof o… │
  │   4 + All operations return (result, ProofObject) pairs.               │
  │   5 +                                                                  │
  │   6 + Mathematical foundation: Menezes, Oorschot, Vanstone — "Handboo… │
  │   7 + Biblical: Proverbs 25:2 — "It is the glory of God to conceal a … │
  │   8 + to search out a matter is the glory of kings."                   │
  │   9 + """                                                              │
  │  10 +                                                                  │
  │  11 + import hashlib                                                   │
  │  12 + from dataclasses import dataclass, field                         │
  │  13 + from fractions import Fraction                                   │
  │  14 + from typing import List, Optional, Tuple                         │
  │  15 +                                                                  │
  │  16 + from axioms.logic import ProofObject                             │
  │  17 +                                                                  │
  │  18 +                                                                  │
  │  19 + @dataclass(frozen=True)                                          │
  │  20 + class HashChainLink:                                             │
  │  21 +     index: int                                                   │
  │  22 +     data: str                                                    │
  │  23 +     previous_hash: str                                           │
  │  24 +     current_hash: str                                            │
  │  25 +                                                                  │
  │  26 +                                                                  │
  │  27 + def sha256_hex(data: str) -> str:                                │
  │  28 +     """Deterministic SHA-256 hex digest."""                      │
  │  29 +     return hashlib.sha256(data.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()      │
  │  30 +                                                                  │
  │  31 +                                                                  │
  │  32 + def build_hash_chain(entries: List[str]) -> Tuple[List[HashChai… │
  │  33 +     """Build a hash chain from a list of string entries.         │
  │  34 +                                                                  │
  │  35 +     Each link's hash = SHA-256(previous_hash + data).            │
  │  36 +     Returns the chain and a proof of construction.               │
  │  37 +     """                                                          │
  │  38 +     chain: List[HashChainLink] = []                              │
  │  39 +     prev = "0" * 64  # Genesis hash                              │
  │  40 +                                                                  │
  │  41 +     for i, entry in enumerate(entries):                          │
  │  42 +         current = sha256_hex(prev + entry)                       │
  │  43 +         chain.append(HashChainLink(                              │
  │  44 +             index=i, data=entry,                                 │
  │  45 +             previous_hash=prev, current_hash=current,            │
  │  46 +         ))                                                       │
  │  47 +         prev = current                                           │
  │  48 +                                                                  │
  │  49 +     proof = ProofObject(                                         │
  │  50 +         conclusion=f"Hash chain of length {len(chain)} construc… │
  │  51 +         premises=[f"Link {c.index}: {c.current_hash[:16]}..." f… │
  │  52 +         rule="hash_chain_construction",                          │
  │  53 +     )                                                            │
